# Annex VII – Gender Assessment

## Green Climate Fund Funding Priorities v2.0

Through usual analysis, data has been collated to establish a baseline. This data shall be monitored and evaluated throughout the project lifecycle to ensure that gender equality is promoted and maintained. The differences between men and women within all target populations will be assessed.

### Indicators

1. Number of women and other vulnerable groups included in project consultations, and number of women and other vulnerable groups who participated.
2. Number of women and other vulnerable groups engaged in adaptation and resilience activities.
3. Number of women and other vulnerable groups who have benefited from the best practices through gender-responsive planning, implementation, and monitoring of climate change adaptation and resilience activities.
4. Number of women and other vulnerable groups who will understand the importance of the community-based management and monitoring activities and be included in the community-based management and monitoring activities.
5. Number of women and other vulnerable groups, including people with disabilities, who are able to access and use the services provided by the project.
6. Outcomes/achievements
7. Improvement in community affairs by women and other vulnerable groups as a result of their participation in the project (economic, social, and civil rights) related outcomes.
8. Awareness of the economic and social impacts of climate change negatively affecting women.

# Gender and Social Inclusion Action Plan

This Gender Action Plan provides a summary of the actions that UNICEF will undertake to ensure that its programmes are gender responsive and track progress on how these actions are implemented. This plan is intended as the standard UNDAF one which will be developed as part of the annual planning process. It will be reviewed annually in line with the standard UNDAF cycle. The action plan will be developed by the end of each calendar year to ensure that it is aligned with the overall strategic objectives and the annual results framework.

## Key Results and Targets

### Inputs: Resources, Policies, and Legislation
- **Policy and legal frameworks** are in place to promote gender equality.
- **Gender-responsive budgeting** is implemented at all levels of government.
- **Monitoring and evaluation systems** are in place to track progress.

### Outputs: Services and Infrastructure
- **Gender-responsive services** are provided to women, girls, boys, and men.
- **Gender-responsive infrastructure** is constructed or renovated.
- **Gender-responsive communication strategies** are implemented.

### Outcomes: Gender Equality
- **Gender equality** is achieved in education, health, and other sectors.
- **Gender-based violence** is reduced by 50%.
- **Gender equality** is promoted through community engagement.

## Domestic Statement

### Commitments
- **Establishment of integrated social funds**: UNDAF Establishes an Integrated Social Fund (ISF) to support gender-responsive interventions in health, education, and other sectors.
- **Gender-responsive budgeting**: UNDAF supports the government's efforts to implement gender-responsive budgeting at all levels.

### Indicators
- **Percentage of women** participating in decision-making processes.
- **Number of gender-responsive projects** implemented.
- **Budget allocated for gender equality**.

## Overall Commitments

| Outcome | Indicators and Targets | Entities | Responsibilities | Timeframe |
|---------|------------------------|----------|-------------------|-----------|
| 1. Year of the Child, 2019: Launch of the National Action Plan on Children's Rights | UNICEF, Government, Civil Society Organizations (CSOs) | UNICEF, Government, CSOs | Launch and monitor progress | Annual |
| 2. Gender Equality in Education: Increase enrollment of girls in primary schools | Ministry of Education (MoE), UNICEF | MoE, UNICEF | Implement and monitor progress | Annual |
| 3. Gender Equality in Health: Reduce maternal mortality ratio by 50% | Ministry of Health (MoH), UNICEF | MoH, UNICEF | Implement and monitor progress | Annual |

## Component 1: Establishment of Integrated Zone Zone Management (ICZM)

### Commitments
- **Establishment of integrated zone management**: UNDAF supports the establishment of an Integrated Coastal Zone Management (ICZM) plan.
- **Capacity building for local communities**: UNDAF provides training and resources to local communities.

### Indicators
- **Number of communities** participating in ICZM planning.
- **Budget allocated for ICZM projects**.
- **Number of trained community members**.

### Responsibilities
- **Local governments**, **NGOs**, **UNICEF**

### Timeframe
- **Every year**
